 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark
     3	 * 
     4	 * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response
     5	 * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light
     6	 * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:
     7	 * 
     8	 * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry
     9	 * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)
    10	 * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values
    11	 * 
    12	 * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.
    13	 */
    14	
    15	#include <algorithm>
    16	#include <array>
    17	#include <chrono>
    18	#include <cmath>
    19	#include <cstdint>
    20	#include <cstdio>
    21	#include <cstdlib>
    22	#include <cstring>
    23	#include <limits>
    24	#include <map>
    25	#include <memory>
    26	#include <random>
    27	#include <vector>

    35	using idx_t = uint32_t;
    36	using val_t = float;
    37	
    38	constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
    39	constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
    40	constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
    41	constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
    42	constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
    43	constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
    44	constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
    45	constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;

    48	// Vector and Triangle Types

    51	struct Vec3 {
    52	    val_t x, y, z;
    53	
    54	    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    55	    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    56	    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}
    57	
    58	    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    59	    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    60	    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    61	    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }
    62	    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }
    63	
    64	    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    65	    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {
    66	        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
    70	    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }
    71	    Vec3 normalized() const {
    72	        val_t n = norm();
    73	        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {
    77	        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;
    78	    }
    79	};
    80	
    81	struct Triangle {
    82	    Vec3 a, b, c;
    83	    Vec3 _normal;
    84	
    85	    Triangle() = default;
    86	    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)
    87	        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}
    88	
    89	    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }
    90	    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }
    91	
    92	    val_t area() const {
    93	        Vec3 ab = b - a;
    94	        Vec3 ac = c - a;
    95	        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }
    99	};

   101	// ============================================================================
   102	// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)
   103	// ============================================================================
   104	
   105	// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap
   106	bool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {
   107	    // Translate triangle to box center
   108	    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;
   109	    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;
   110	    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;
   111	
   112	    // Triangle edges
   113	    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;
   114	    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;
   115	    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;
   116	
   117	    // Test box normals (AABB axes)
   118	    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {
   119	        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));
   120	    };
   121	
   122	    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);
   123	    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;
   124	
   125	    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);
   126	    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;
   127	
   128	    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);
   129	    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;
   130	
   131	    // Test triangle normal
   132	    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);
   133	    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);
   134	    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +
   135	              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +
   136	              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);
   137	    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;
   138	
   139	    // Test 9 edge cross products
   140	    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {
   141	        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);
   142	        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);
   143	        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);
   144	        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +
   145	                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +
   146	                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);
   147	        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);
   148	        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);
   149	    };
   150	
   151	    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};
   152	    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};
   153	    for (const auto& axis : axes) {
   154	        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
   155	            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);
   156	            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {
   157	                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;
   158	            }
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    return true;
   163	}
   164	
   165	// ============================================================================
   166	// Octree for Spatial Acceleration
   167	// ============================================================================
   168	
   169	constexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;
   170	constexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;
   171	
   172	class Octree {
   173	public:
   174	    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;
   175	    Vec3 halfExtent, center;
   176	    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];
   177	    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list
   178	    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles
   179	
   180	    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}
   181	
   182	    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {
   183	        allTriangles = &triangles;
   184	        if (triangles.empty()) return;
   185	
   186	        // Compute bounding box
   187	        minBound = triangles[0].a;
   188	        maxBound = triangles[0].a;
   189	        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {
   190	            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {
   191	                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);
   192	                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);
   193	                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);
   194	                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);
   195	                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);
   196	                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);
   197	            }
   198	        }
   199	
   200	        // Collect all indices
   201	        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());
   202	        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;
   203	
   204	        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);
   205	    }
   206	
   207	private:
   208	    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {
   209	        minBound = nodeMin;
   210	        maxBound = nodeMax;
   211	        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;
   212	        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;
   213	
   214	        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf
   215	        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||
   216	            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {
   217	            triangleIndices = indices;
   218	            return;
   219	        }
   220	
   221	        // Subdivide into 8 children
   222	        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;
   223	        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];
   224	
   225	        for (size_t idx : indices) {
   226	            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];
   227	
   228	            // Check which children this triangle overlaps
   229	            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   230	                Vec3 childCenter = center;
   231	                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;
   232	                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;
   233	                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;
   234	
   235	                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {
   236	                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);
   237	                }
   238	            }
   239	        }
   240	
   241	        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)
   242	        bool canSplit = false;
   243	        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   244	            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {
   245	                canSplit = true;
   246	                break;
   247	            }
   248	        }
   249	
   250	        if (!canSplit) {
   251	            triangleIndices = indices;
   252	            return;
   253	        }
   254	
   255	        // Build children
   256	        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   257	            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {
   258	                Vec3 childMin = center;
   259	                Vec3 childMax = center;
   260	                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;
   261	                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;
   262	                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;
   263	                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;
   264	                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;
   265	                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;
   266	
   267	                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();
   268	                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;
   269	                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);
   270	            }
   271	        }
   272	    }
   273	
   274	public:
   275	    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box
   276	    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {
   277	        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;
   278	        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;
   279	        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};
   280	
   281	        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;
   282	        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;
   283	        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;
   284	
   285	        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
   286	        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
   287	        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;
   288	
   289	        return true;
   290	    }
   291	
   292	    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray
   293	    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle
   294	    template<typename Func>
   295	    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {
   296	        // If leaf node, check triangles directly
   297	        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {
   298	            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {
   299	                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;
   300	            }
   301	            return false;
   302	        }
   303	
   304	        // Otherwise, descend to children
   305	        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   306	            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {
   307	                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;
   308	            }
   309	        }
   310	        return false;
   311	    }
   312	};
   313	
   314	// ============================================================================
   315	// Mesh Generation: Icosphere

   318	// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron
   319	class IcosphereMesh {
   320	public:
   321	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   322	
   323	    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {
   324	        // Initial icosahedron vertices
   325	        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;
   326	
   327	        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {
   328	            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
   329	            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
   330	            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
   331	            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
   332	            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,
   333	            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,
   334	            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,
   335	            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,
   336	            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
   337	            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,
   338	            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
   339	            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius
   340	        };
   341	
   342	        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)
   343	        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {
   344	            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},
   345	            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},
   346	            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},
   347	            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}
   348	        };
   349	
   350	        // Subdivide

   771	// ============================================================================
   772	// Hash for Verification
   773	// ============================================================================
   774	
   775	uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
   776	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   777	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   778	        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
   779	        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   780	    }
   781	    return hash;
   782	}
   783	
   784	// ============================================================================
   785	// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
   786	// ============================================================================
   787	
   788	int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
   789	    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
   790	    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
   791	    int subdivisions = 0;
   792	    int triangles = 20;
   793	    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
   794	        subdivisions++;
   795	        triangles *= 4;
   796	    }
   797	    return subdivisions;
   798	}
   799	
   800	// ============================================================================
   801	// Main
   802	// ============================================================================
   803	
   804	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   805	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   806	    printf("Options:\n");
   807	    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
   808	    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
   809	    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
   810	    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
   811	    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
   812	    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
   813	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   814	    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
   815	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   816	}
   817	
   818	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   819	    int targetTriangles = 320;
   820	    int timesteps = 50;
   821	    int sourceIdx = 0;
   822	    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
   823	    bool validate = false;
   824	    bool printResults = false;
   825	
   826	    // Parse command line arguments
   827	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   828	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   829	            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
   830	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   831	            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   832	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   833	            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   834	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   835	            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
   836	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   837	            validate = true;
   838	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
   839	            printResults = true;
   840	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   841	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   842	            return 0;
   843	        } else {
   844	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   845	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   846	            return 1;
   847	        }
   848	    }
   849	
   850	    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);
   851	
   852	    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
   853	    printf("===================================\n");
   854	    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
   855	    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
   856	    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
   857	    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
   858	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   859	    printf("\n");
   860	
   861	    // Initialize
   862	    SimulationState state;
   863	    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
   864	                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);
   865	
   866	    printf("\n");
   867	
   868	    // Precomputation
   869	    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   870	
   871	    computeTimeDelays(state);
   872	    computeFormFactors(state);
   873	
   874	    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   875	    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();
   876	
   877	    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
   878	    printf("\n");
   879	
   880	    // Simulation
   881	    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   882	
   883	    runSimulation(state);
   884	
   885	    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   886	    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();
   887	
   888	    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
   889	    printf("\n");
   890	
   891	    // Distance computation
   892	    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   893	
   894	    computeDistances(state);
   895	
   896	    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   897	    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();
   898	
   899	    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
   900	    printf("\n");
   901	
   902	    // Total time
   903	    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
   904	    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);
   905	
   906	    // Performance metrics
   907	    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
   908	    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
   909	    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
   910	    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
   911	    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);
   912	
   913	    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
   914	    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
   915	    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
   916	    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
   917	    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
   918	    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
   919	    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);
   920	
   921	    // Memory usage
   922	    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
   923	    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
   924	    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
   925	    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
   926	    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   927	
   928	    // Hash
   929	    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
   930	    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
   931	    printf("\n");
   932	    
   933	    // Print results for external validation
   934	    if (printResults) {
   935	        // Convert distances to double for output
   936	        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
   937	        print_results(distData, "Distances");
   938	    }
   939	
   940	    // Validation
   941	    if (validate) {
   942	        if (!validateResults(state)) {
   943	            return 1;
   944	        }
   945	    }
   946	
   947	    return 0;
   948	}

   356	                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));

   390	// ============================================================================
   391	// Random Number Generation
   392	// ============================================================================
   393	
   394	class RandomGenerator {
   395	    std::mt19937 rng;
   396	    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
   397	public:
   398	    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
   399	    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
   400	};
   401	
   402	// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
   403	Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
   404	    val_t u = rng.rand();
   405	    val_t v = rng.rand();
   406	    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
   407	        u = 1.0f - u;
   408	        v = 1.0f - v;
   409	    }
   410	    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
   411	    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
   412	    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
   413	}
   414	
   415	// ============================================================================
   416	// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
   417	// ============================================================================
   418	
   419	val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
   420	                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
   421	    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
   422	    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
   423	    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
   424	    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);
   425	
   426	    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   427	
   428	    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
   429	    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
   430	    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
   431	    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   432	
   433	    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
   434	    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   435	    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   436	
   437	    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   438	}
   439	
   440	// ============================================================================
   441	// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
   442	// ============================================================================
   443	
   444	// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
   445	// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
   446	bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
   447	                  const Octree& octree,
   448	                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
   449	    Vec3 dir = to - from;
   450	    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
   451	    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
   452	    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;
   453	
   454	    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
   455	        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;
   456	
   457	        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
   458	        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
   459	            return true;  // Ray is blocked
   460	        }
   461	        return false;
   462	    });
   463	}
   464	
   465	// ============================================================================
   466	// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
   467	// ============================================================================
   468	
   469	// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
   470	val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
   471	    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
   472	    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
   473	    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
   474	}
   475	
   476	// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
   477	val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
   478	                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
   479	                  const Octree& octree,
   480	                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
   481	    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
   482	    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];
   483	
   484	    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
   485	    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;
   486	
   487	    val_t kij = ZERO;
   488	
   489	    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
   490	        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
   491	        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);
   492	
   493	        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;
   494	
   495	        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
   496	        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
   497	        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
   498	
   499	        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
   500	        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());
   501	
   502	        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;
   503	
   504	        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
   508	}
   509	
   510	// ============================================================================
   511	// Tau (time delay) Computation
   512	// ============================================================================
   513	
   514	int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
   515	    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
   516	    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
   517	}
   518	
   519	// ============================================================================
   520	// Room Response Simulation State
   521	// ============================================================================
   522	
   523	struct SimulationState {
   524	    size_t numTriangles;
   525	    size_t numTimesteps;
   526	
   527	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   528	    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
   529	    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
   530	    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
   531	    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
   532	    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
   533	    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
   534	    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source
   535	
   536	    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure
   537	
   538	    size_t sourceIndex;
   539	
   540	    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
   541	    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
   542	};
   543	
   544	// ============================================================================
   545	// Initialization
   546	// ============================================================================
   547	
   548	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   549	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
   550	    // Generate mesh
   551	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   552	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   553	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   554	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   555	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   556	
   557	    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   558	
   559	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   560	    printf("Building octree...\n");
   561	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   562	
   563	    // Initialize areas
   564	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   565	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   566	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   567	    }
   568	
   569	    // Initialize reflectivity
   570	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   571	
   572	    // Initialize matrices
   573	    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   574	    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
   575	    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   576	    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   577	    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   578	
   579	    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
   580	    size_t timeOn = 0;
   581	    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
   582	    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
   583	        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
   584	    }
   585	}
   586	
   587	// ============================================================================
   588	// Precomputation Phase
   589	// ============================================================================
   590	
   591	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   592	    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
   593	    RandomGenerator rng(42);
   594	
   595	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   596	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   597	            if (i == j) continue;
   598	            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
   599	                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   600	        }
   601	        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
   602	            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
   603	        }
   604	    }
   605	}
   606	
   607	void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
   608	    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
   609	
   610	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   611	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   612	            if (i == j) continue;
   613	            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(
   614	                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
   615	        }
   616	    }
   617	}
   618	
   619	// ============================================================================
   620	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   621	// ============================================================================
   622	
   623	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
   624	    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
   625	
   626	    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   627	        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   628	            val_t sumB = ZERO;
   629	
   630	            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   631	                if (i == j) continue;
   632	
   633	                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];
   634	
   635	                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
   636	                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;
   637	
   638	                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
   639	                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
   640	
   641	                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
   642	                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
   643	                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
   644	                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   645	
   646	                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
   647	                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
   648	            }
   649	
   650	            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
   651	            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
   652	        }
   653	
   654	        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
   655	            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
   656	        }
   657	    }
   658	}
   659	
   660	// ============================================================================
   661	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   662	// ============================================================================
   663	
   664	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   665	    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   666	
   667	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   668	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   669	        int bestT = 0;
   670	
   671	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   672	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   673	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   674	
   675	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   676	                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
   677	                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
   678	                sum += pS * pB;
   679	            }
   680	
   681	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   682	                maxCorr = sum;
   683	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   684	            }
   685	        }
   686	
   687	        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   688	    }
   689	}
   690	
   691	// ============================================================================
   692	// Validation
   693	// ============================================================================
   694	
   695	bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
   696	    printf("\nValidation:\n");
   697	
   698	    // Check that distances are non-negative
   699	    bool allNonNegative = true;
   700	    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   701	    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   702	    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
   703	    int nonZeroCount = 0;
   704	
   705	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   706	        val_t d = state.distances[i];
   707	        if (d < 0) {
   708	            allNonNegative = false;
   709	            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   710	        }
   711	        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
   712	            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   713	            return false;
   714	        }
   715	        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
   716	        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
   717	        sumDist += d;
   718	        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
   719	    }
   720

// ============================================================================
// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
// ============================================================================

val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);

    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
}

// ============================================================================
// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
// ============================================================================

// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
    Vec3 dir = to - from;
    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;

    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;

        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
            return true;  // Ray is blocked
        }
        return false;
    });
}

// ============================================================================
// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
// ============================================================================

// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
}

// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];

    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;

    val_t kij = ZERO;

    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);

        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;

        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;

        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());

        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;

        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
    }

    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
}

// ============================================================================
// Tau (time delay) Computation
// ============================================================================

int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
}

     1	/**
     2	 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark
     3	 * 
     4	 * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response
     5	 * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light
     6	 * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:
     7	 * 
     8	 * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry
     9	 * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)
    10	 * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values
    11	 * 
    12	 * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.
    13	 */

    37	
    38	constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
    39	constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
    40	constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
    41	constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
    42	constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
    43	constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
    44	constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;

    47	// ============================================================================
    48	// Vector and Triangle Types

    50	
    51	struct Vec3 {
    52	    val_t x, y, z;
    53	
    54	    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    55	    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    56	    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}
    57	
    58	    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    59	    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    60	    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    61	    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }
    62	    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }
    63	
    64	    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    65	    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {
    66	        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
    70	    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }
    71	    Vec3 normalized() const {
    72	        val_t n = norm();
    73	        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {
    77	        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;
    78	    }
    79	};
    80	
    81	struct Triangle {
    82	    Vec3 a, b, c;
    83	    Vec3 _normal;
    84	
    85	    Triangle() = default;
    86	    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)
    87	        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}
    88	
    89	    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }
    90	    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }
    91	
    92	    val_t area() const {
    93	        Vec3 ab = b - a;
    94	        Vec3 ac = c - a;
    95	        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }
    99	};
   100	
   101	// ============================================================================
   102	// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)
   103	// ============================================================================
   104	
   105	// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap
   106	bool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {
   107	    // Translate triangle to box center
   108	    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;
   109	    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;
   110	    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;
   111	
   112	    // Triangle edges
   113	    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;
   114	    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;
   115	    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;
   116	
   117	    // Test box normals (AABB axes)
   118	    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {
   119	        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));
   120	    };
   121	
   122	    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);
   123	    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;
   124	
   125	    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);
   126	    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;
   127	
   128	    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);
   129	    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;
   130	
   131	    // Test triangle normal
   132	    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);
   133	    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);
   134	    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +
   135	              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +
   136	              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);
   137	    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;
   138	
   139	    // Test 9 edge cross products
   140	    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {
   141	        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);
   142	        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);
   143	        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);
   144	        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +
   145	                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +
   146	                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);
   147	        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);
   148	        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);
   149	    };
   150	
   151	    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};
   152	    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};
   153	    for (const auto& axis : axes) {
   154	        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
   155	            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);
   156	            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {
   157	                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;
   158	            }
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    return true;
   163	}
   164	
   165	// ============================================================================
   166	// Octree for Spatial Acceleration
   167	// ============================================================================
   168	
   169	constexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;
   170	constexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;
   171	
   172	class Octree {
   173	public:
   174	    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;
   175	    Vec3 halfExtent, center;
   176	    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];
   177	    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list
   178	    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles
   179	
   180	    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}
   181	
   182	    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {
   183	        allTriangles = &triangles;
   184	        if (triangles.empty()) return;
   185	
   186	        // Compute bounding box
   187	        minBound = triangles[0].a;
   188	        maxBound = triangles[0].a;
   189	        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {
   190	            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {
   191	                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);
   192	                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);
   193	                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);
   194	                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);
   195	                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);
   196	                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);
   197	            }
   198	        }
   199	
   200	        // Collect all indices
   201	        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());
   202	        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;
   203	
   204	        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);
   205	    }
   206	
   207	private:
   208	    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {
   209	        minBound = nodeMin;
   210	        maxBound = nodeMax;
   211	        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;
   212	        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;
   213	
   214	        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf
   215	        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||
   216	            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {
   217	            triangleIndices = indices;
   218	            return;
   219	        }
   220	
   221	        // Subdivide into 8 children
   222	        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;
   223	        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];
   224	
   225	        for (size_t idx : indices) {
   226	            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];
   227	
   228	            // Check which children this triangle overlaps
   229	            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   230	                Vec3 childCenter = center;
   231	                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;
   232	                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;
   233	                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;
   234	
   235	                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {
   236	                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);
   237	                }
   238	            }
   239	        }
   240	
   241	        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)
   242	        bool canSplit = false;
   243	        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   244	            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {
   245	                canSplit = true;
   246	                break;
   247	            }
   248	        }
   249	
   250	        if (!canSplit) {
   251	            triangleIndices = indices;
   252	            return;
   253	        }
   254	
   255	        // Build children
   256	        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   257	            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {
   258	                Vec3 childMin = center;
   259	                Vec3 childMax = center;
   260	                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;
   261	                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;
   262	                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;
   263	                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;
   264	                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;
   265	                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;
   266	
   267	                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();
   268	                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;
   269	                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);
   270	            }
   271	        }
   272	    }
   273	
   274	public:
   275	    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box
   276	    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {
   277	        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;
   278	        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;
   279	        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};
   280	
   281	        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;
   282	        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;
   283	        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;
   284	
   285	        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;
   286	        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;
   287	        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;
   288	
   289	        return true;
   290	    }
   291	
   292	    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray
   293	    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle
   294	    template<typename Func>
   295	    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {
   296	        // If leaf node, check triangles directly
   297	        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {
   298	            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {
   299	                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;
   300	            }
   301	            return false;
   302	        }
   303	
   304	        // Otherwise, descend to children
   305	        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
   306	            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {
   307	                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;
   308	            }
   309	        }
   310	        return false;
   311	    }
   312	};
   313

   771	// ============================================================================
   772	// Hash for Verification
   773	// ============================================================================
   774	
   775	uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
   776	    uint64_t hash = 0;
   777	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   778	        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
   779	        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   780	    }
   781	    return hash;
   782	}

   784	// ============================================================================
   785	// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
   786	// ============================================================================
   787	
   788	int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
   789	    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
   790	    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
   791	    int subdivisions = 0;
   792	    int triangles = 20;
   793	    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
   794	        subdivisions++;
   795	        triangles *= 4;
   796	    }
   797	    return subdivisions;
   798	}

   909	    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);

   920

   922	    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
   923	    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
   924	    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);

   927

   944	        }
   945	    }
   946	
   947	    return 0;

   355	            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {
   356	                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));
   357	                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);

   390	// ============================================================================
   391	// Random Number Generation
   392	// ============================================================================
   393	
   394	class RandomGenerator {
   395	    std::mt19937 rng;
   396	    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
   397	public:
   398	    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
   399	    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
   400	};
   401	
   402	// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
   403	Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
   404	    val_t u = rng.rand();
   405	    val_t v = rng.rand();
   406	    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
   407	        u = 1.0f - u;
   408	        v = 1.0f - v;
   409	    }
   410	    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
   411	    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
   412	    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
   413	}
   414	
   415	// ============================================================================
   416	// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
   417	// ============================================================================
   418	
   419	val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
   420	                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
   421	    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
   422	    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
   423	    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
   424	    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);
   425	
   426	    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   427	
   428	    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
   429	    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
   430	    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
   431	    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   432	
   433	    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
   434	    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   435	    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   436	
   437	    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
   438	}
   439	
   440	// ============================================================================
   441	// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
   442	// ============================================================================
   443	
   444	// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
   445	// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
   446	bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
   447	                  const Octree& octree,
   448	                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
   449	    Vec3 dir = to - from;
   450	    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
   451	    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
   452	    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;
   453	
   454	    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
   455	        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;
   456	
   457	        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
   458	        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
   459	            return true;  // Ray is blocked
   460	        }
   461	        return false;
   462	    });
   463	}
   464	
   465	// ============================================================================
   466	// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
   467	// ============================================================================
   468	
   469	// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
   470	val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
   471	    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
   472	    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
   473	    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
   474	}
   475	
   476	// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
   477	val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
   478	                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
   479	                  const Octree& octree,
   480	                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
   481	    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
   482	    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];
   483	
   484	    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
   485	    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;
   486	
   487	    val_t kij = ZERO;
   488	
   489	    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
   490	        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
   491	        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);
   492	
   493	        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;
   494	
   495	        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
   496	        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
   497	        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
   498	
   499	        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
   500	        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());
   501	
   502	        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;
   503	
   504	        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
   508	}
   509	
   510	// ============================================================================
   511	// Tau (time delay) Computation
   512	// ============================================================================
   513	
   514	int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
   515	    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
   516	    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
   517	}
   518	
   519	// ============================================================================
   520	// Room Response Simulation State
   521	// ============================================================================
   522	
   523	struct SimulationState {
   524	    size_t numTriangles;
   525	    size_t numTimesteps;
   526	
   527	    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
   528	    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
   529	    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
   530	    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
   531	    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
   532	    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
   533	    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
   534	    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source
   535	
   536	    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure
   537	
   538	    size_t sourceIndex;
   539	
   540	    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
   541	    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
   542	};
   543	
   544	// ============================================================================
   545	// Initialization
   546	// ============================================================================
   547	
   548	void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
   549	                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
   550	    // Generate mesh
   551	    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
   552	    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
   553	    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
   554	    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
   555	    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
   556	
   557	    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
   558	
   559	    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
   560	    printf("Building octree...\n");
   561	    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
   562	
   563	    // Initialize areas
   564	    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
   565	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   566	        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
   567	    }
   568	
   569	    // Initialize reflectivity
   570	    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
   571	
   572	    // Initialize matrices
   573	    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   574	    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
   575	    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   576	    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   577	    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
   578	
   579	    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
   580	    size_t timeOn = 0;
   581	    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
   582	    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
   583	        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
   584	    }
   585	}
   586	
   587	// ============================================================================
   588	// Precomputation Phase
   589	// ============================================================================
   590	
   591	void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
   592	    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
   593	    RandomGenerator rng(42);
   594	
   595	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   596	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   597	            if (i == j) continue;
   598	            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
   599	                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
   600	        }
   601	        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
   602	            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
   603	        }
   604	    }
   605	}
   606	
   607	void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
   608	    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
   609	
   610	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   611	        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   612	            if (i == j) continue;
   613	            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(
   614	                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
   615	        }
   616	    }
   617	}
   618	
   619	// ============================================================================
   620	// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
   621	// ============================================================================
   622	
   623	void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
   624	    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
   625	
   626	    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   627	        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   628	            val_t sumB = ZERO;
   629	
   630	            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
   631	                if (i == j) continue;
   632	
   633	                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];
   634	
   635	                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
   636	                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;
   637	
   638	                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
   639	                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
   640	
   641	                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
   642	                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
   643	                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
   644	                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
   645	
   646	                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
   647	                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
   648	            }
   649	
   650	            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
   651	            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
   652	        }
   653	
   654	        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
   655	            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
   656	        }
   657	    }
   658	}
   659	
   660	// ============================================================================
   661	// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
   662	// ============================================================================
   663	
   664	void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
   665	    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
   666	
   667	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   668	        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
   669	        int bestT = 0;
   670	
   671	        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
   672	        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
   673	            val_t sum = ZERO;
   674	
   675	            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
   676	                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
   677	                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
   678	                sum += pS * pB;
   679	            }
   680	
   681	            if (sum > maxCorr) {
   682	                maxCorr = sum;
   683	                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
   684	            }
   685	        }
   686	
   687	        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
   688	    }
   689	}
   690	
   691	// ============================================================================
   692	// Validation
   693	// ============================================================================
   694	
   695	bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
   696	    printf("\nValidation:\n");
   697	
   698	    // Check that distances are non-negative
   699	    bool allNonNegative = true;
   700	    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   701	    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   702	    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
   703	    int nonZeroCount = 0;
   704	
   705	    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
   706	        val_t d = state.distances[i];
   707	        if (d < 0) {
   708	            allNonNegative = false;
   709	            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   710	        }
   711	        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
   712	            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
   713	            return false;
   714	        }
   715	        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
   716	        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
   717	        sumDist += d;
   718	        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
   719	    }
   720

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

from pathlib import Path

s=p.read_text()
